# Agents · Q · part 5

940 entries, qa-reviewer to qa-validator. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **qa-reviewer**: 敵対的QA（Adversarial QA）として、対象コードを例外終了・失敗させる破壊テストを作成・実行して客観評価し、テストコードのトートロジー（実装式コピペ）や甘いアサーションを容赦なく暴く専門サブエージェント。 ([TakefumiSugeno/AIUtils](https://github.com/TakefumiSugeno/AIUtils/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/qa-reviewer.md) · 3 copies)
- **qa-reviewer**: &gt; **版本**: v1.0.0 | **更新日期**: 2026-01-08 | **環境**: VS Code + GitHub Copilot ([TanaChu0409/book-keeper](https://github.com/TanaChu0409/book-keeper/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/qa-reviewer.md))
- **qa-reviewer**: Reviews test, lint, and typecheck evidence supplied by the Primary Agent, audits git diffs, and issues definitive quality gate decisions. ([Tayyabthecoder/Agent-Kernel](https://github.com/Tayyabthecoder/Agent-Kernel/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/qa-reviewer.md))
- **qa-reviewer**: Quality assurance and adversarial reviewer for data science projects. Checks for data leakage, bias, overfitting, methodology errors, and code quality. Use this agent to critically review analysis, models, and pipelines before presenting to stakeholders. ([thbraet/claude-ralph-tutorial](https://github.com/thbraet/claude-ralph-tutorial/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/qa-reviewer.md) · 2 copies)
- **qa-reviewer**: Adversarial reviewer for Playwright specs produced by test-writer. Runs the specs, reads the output, and either approves them for commit or returns a structured critique listing every defect. Use this agent immediately after test-writer reports SPEC WRITTEN. It is the gate between test-writer and the main commit thread. ([timothywarner-org/pluralsight-author-talk-ai-agents](https://github.com/timothywarner-org/pluralsight-author-talk-ai-agents/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/qa-reviewer.md))
- **qa-reviewer**: QA expert and code reviewer. Use after an implementation, before merging. Checks the diff against the acceptance criteria, runs the tests and reports concrete findings. Does not rewrite code. ([tomonfire/template](https://github.com/tomonfire/template/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/qa-reviewer.md))
- **qa-reviewer**: Revisor de calidad de solo lectura para los proyectos Iori. Úsalo siempre al terminar un workstream o antes de dar algo por hecho, para verificar builds, referencias huérfanas, configs duplicadas, compatibilidad 1.8.9–1.21 y seguridad del bot de Discord. ([Tqmass/IoriCore](https://github.com/Tqmass/IoriCore/blob/HEAD/.cursor/agents/qa-reviewer.md))
- **qa-reviewer**: Revisa tests, scripts, configs y quality gates y devuelve findings BLOCKER/HIGH/MEDIUM/LOW sin modificar nada. ([trombonet12/Rally-Copilot](https://github.com/trombonet12/Rally-Copilot/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/qa-reviewer.md) · 2 copies)
- **qa-reviewer**: Reviews one Antigravity pull request (branch ag/*) against docs/agents/qa-checklist.md and ends with a single QA verdict line. Use when asked to QA an Antigravity PR. ([tubeoism/secure-mail-ring](https://github.com/tubeoism/secure-mail-ring/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/qa-reviewer.md))
- **qa-reviewer**: Use to assess test coverage and quality of changed code — coverage gaps, untested acceptance criteria, missing edge-case and error-path tests, weak assertions, tests that bind to implementation. Read-only; reports findings, never writes tests. Use during /review and /audit. ([webmint/mintEnvoy](https://github.com/webmint/mintEnvoy/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/qa-reviewer.md) · 2 copies)
- **qa-reviewer**: Independent requirements, correctness, security, and test-quality review after deterministic gates pass. ([wenzelt/omega-software-factory](https://github.com/wenzelt/omega-software-factory/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/qa-reviewer.md))
- **qa-reviewer**: 동화책 통합 검증자. 시나리오·프롬프트·이미지·뷰어가 일관되게 연결되는지 경계면을 교차 비교한다. 단순 존재 확인이 아니라 시나리오 장면과 이미지/뷰어 페이지가 정확히 매칭되는지 검증한다. ([wkdghdrhks/moonlight-library](https://github.com/wkdghdrhks/moonlight-library/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/qa-reviewer.md))
- **qa-reviewer**: ChordLens 코드 품질 검토 전문가. TypeScript 타입 안전성, FSD 아키텍처 준수, API 에러 처리, 코드 컨벤션을 검증한다. code-review 스킬을 사용한다. ([xmun74/chordlens](https://github.com/xmun74/chordlens/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/qa-reviewer.md))
- **qa-reviewer**: BE QA 최종 검증. API 계약, pytest, 레이어 아키텍처 교차 검증. _workspace/05_qa_report.md 작성. ([xmun74/chordlens-be](https://github.com/xmun74/chordlens-be/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/qa-reviewer.md))
- **qa-reviewer**: QA エンジニア役。release-gate や大きめの機能 PR で「欲しかった機能が揃っているか」「変な動きをしないか」を受け入れ観点で検証するときに使う。実装セッションのコンテキストを引き継がず、.github/claude/qa-rubric.md に従って受け入れマトリクスを作り、新規の自動シナリオは外部故障の異常系スモーク (実配線ハーネス e2e-uc) に限って作成・実行し、正常系のゴールデンパス・UI 挙動は実環境 E2E の結果確認と hop 追加提案 (恒常追加は人間裁定) で判定して QA レポートを返す。プロダクトコードは変更しない (触って良いのはテストコードのみ)。 ([yomote/mind-inbox](https://github.com/yomote/mind-inbox/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/qa-reviewer.md))
- **qa-reviewer**: 品質確認の担当。PR やブランチの差分レビュー、テスト・カバレッジ・lint・ビルドの実行、ブラウザでの動作確認、不具合の調査、改善候補の洗い出しを行う。ソースコードは変更せず、所見を返す。 ([yusuke-iw/self-finnish-learner](https://github.com/yusuke-iw/self-finnish-learner/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/qa-reviewer.md))
- **qa-reviewer**: Especialista em QA e Testes automatizados. Avalia cobertura, cenários de borda, mocks abusivos, legibilidade e atribui o QA Score do PR. ([zatzk/spec-harness-kit](https://github.com/zatzk/spec-harness-kit/blob/HEAD/agents/qa-reviewer.md))
- **qa-reviewer**: The planner and judge of a QA verification. Phase plan writes one evidence plan per spec criterion on the evidence ladder (real screen, fixture screen, stub, test, route, doc, manual), adds advisory rows from the risk axes, and writes charter.md, expectations.json, sad-path map entries and mutants that name their killing test; it fixes the plan check's problems once. Phase judge completes the script-made verdict draft into verdict.md, a fresh judge each round that keeps the rows carried from earlier rounds as given, default REJECT. Reads the spec and run summaries, never feature code during j… ([ztsia/workflow](https://github.com/ztsia/workflow/blob/HEAD/plugins/workflow/agents/qa-reviewer.md))
- **qa-reviewer**: Independent QA engineer. Derives acceptance criteria and user flows from the spec, audits whether existing tests actually test anything, exercises backend functions and endpoints, then walks the flows end-to-end in a real browser with screenshots — happy and sad paths — and reports what breaks. Use to verify a module branch before it opens a PR. ([ztsia/zetix](https://github.com/ztsia/zetix/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/qa-reviewer.md))
- **qa-reviewer-1**: Use this agent to verify a Trello card's implementation against the test cases already written for it by qa-conftest, qa-playwright, and qa-robot-framework, and render a satisfied/not-satisfied verdict. It does not write test cases itself and does not fix defects itself. - **qa-revisor**: Use este agente para revisão independente de uma implementação já feita, antes de qualquer ação Git. Ele tenta ativamente provar que a mudança está errada antes de aprová-la — nunca é o autor da implementação que está revisando. ([telurica-digital/M-todo-Ori](https://github.com/telurica-digital/M-todo-Ori/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/qa-revisor.md))
- **qa-revisor-residapp**: Revisor independiente de calidad de ResidApp. Parte de los requisitos (historias de usuario, flujos clínicos, ADR, respuestas de CJ), no del código, y comprueba si las pruebas existentes los cubren; propone los escenarios que faltan (estados, límites, permisos, otro centro) y señala posibles defectos. Úsalo al cerrar un bloque funcional, antes de darlo por bueno, o para preguntar «¿qué requisitos de X no tienen prueba?». Solo lee e informa; no edita ni ejecuta. ([orlando6608/residapp](https://github.com/orlando6608/residapp/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/qa-revisor-residapp.md))
- **qa-risk-analyst**: Risk & regression analyst for the team-qa process. Evaluates what the change could break, which invariants must hold, and how it maps onto this project's known recurring failure modes (if it has a defect catalog configured). Names the "ships green but broken" traps so the test plan closes them. Read-only investigation plus a written findings file. Generic — works on any project. ([saralafleur/ember-claude-toolkit](https://github.com/saralafleur/ember-claude-toolkit/blob/HEAD/plugins/delivery-team/agents/qa-risk-analyst.md) · ★4 · 9 copies)
- **qa-risk-assessor**: Analyzes codebases for testing risk using objective metrics. Identifies untested critical paths, complex logic, high-churn areas, and external dependencies. Scores risk by business impact and technical likelihood. Used by QA orchestrator for discovery phase. ([MattVOLTA/outworkos_diy](https://github.com/MattVOLTA/outworkos_diy/blob/HEAD/skills/plugins/devops-tools/agents/qa-risk-assessor.md) · ★4)
- **qa-risk-classifier**: QA risk classifier — computes Impact x Likelihood plus a capped modifier, applies the parent skill floor rules, and outputs the final level (Low / Medium / High / Critical) with evidence confidence. Never guesses scores: always cites repo evidence such as changed files, git diff, route classification or package presence. ([SensLiao/Claude-code-setting](https://github.com/SensLiao/Claude-code-setting/blob/HEAD/agents/qa-risk-classifier.md))
- **qa-roaster**: Ruthless QA tester and code/infrastructure reviewer. Use proactively after code changes to find bugs, security issues, bad patterns, and infrastructure misconfigurations. - **QA-run-orchestrator**: Test-run triage agent. Runs the configured test suite against the test environment, re-runs failures, classifies each as product-bug, test-bug, flaky or environment (with evidence and a confidence), checks the tracker for existing reports, and files or comments on bugs for real product defects only (via the Bug-reporter agent, with its human review gate). Everything else is reported, never filed. Use when you want a run's failures understood, not just listed. ([Tech9Apps/t9-foundry](https://github.com/Tech9Apps/t9-foundry/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/QA-run-orchestrator.md))
- **qa-runner**: Runs real QA scenarios through the shell, saves output only to parent-named evidence paths, and reports results with cleanup receipts. No file-editing tools. ([wjgoarxiv/litgravity](https://github.com/wjgoarxiv/litgravity/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/qa-runner.md) · ★168)
- **qa-runner**: QA agent for the sdd-autopilot pipeline. Validates a feature implementation by running the test suite, linting, and type checking on the feature's changed files, then writes a structured .autopilot/qa-report.md with a machine-greppable `verdict: PASS | FAIL`. It does NOT fix code — it reports. Fixing is sdd-worker's job. Runs inside the feature worktree, read + shell only (no edits). Examples: Context: sdd-autopilot reaches Stage 3 (QA) after code review passes. user: "Run test suite for FEAT-071. Output report to .autopilot/qa-report.md with verdict: PASS | FAIL" assistant: "I'll run pytest… ([phenobarbital/asyncdb](https://github.com/phenobarbital/asyncdb/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/qa-runner.md) · ★55 · 10 copies)
- **qa-runner**: Executes one or more structured QA tests (from qa/tests/structured/*.yaml) in order against a specified iOS simulator, writing results, criteria, state, logs, events, bugs, accessibility findings, and performance data to CXDB. - **qa-runner**: Tests a Gameface view running in the Gameface Player against a spec's done-criteria, or against what the prompt asks for - screenshots, layout assertions, interactions, console errors. Use when asked to QA or test a Gameface view. Performance is gameface-reviewer's job. Reports only; never edits files. Needs the gameface MCP server. - **qa-runner**: Scope-deciding QA agent. Reads the change set (a branch/worktree or a range), picks the targeted checks per CLAUDE/QA.md, runs them and reports VERDICT, SCOPE, REASONING and FAILURES. Read-only; never fixes and never runs the full suite (a release step for the main thread). ([Edmonds-Commerce-Limited/claude-code-hooks-daemon](https://github.com/Edmonds-Commerce-Limited/claude-code-hooks-daemon/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/qa-runner.md) · ★4)
- **qa-runner**: Plans a short Playwright script (route, role, steps, expected state) against the local app stack from a diff and the app's route map. Does not execute browser automation itself — it writes the script for a no-model runner to execute. Use only for the planning half of UI evidence generation. ([jthorare/agentic-workflow](https://github.com/jthorare/agentic-workflow/blob/HEAD/config/agents/qa-runner.md) · ★3)
- **qa-runner**: Self-contained agent-invoked entry point for a walkthrough — takes a brief instead of asking questions, runs the full six-phase pipeline itself, and returns the finished report or pauses per the escalation mechanism. Use when another agent needs to run agentic-qa unattended and doesn't hold the toolset (Agent, SendMessage) to call agentic-qa:agentic-qa directly. The caller needs SendMessage to resume it after an escalation. ([bobtat/claude-plugins](https://github.com/bobtat/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/agentic-qa/agents/qa-runner.md) · ★2)
- **qa-runner**: You are the autonomous QA agent for the slide-maker app. You execute testing, collect structured diagnostic logs, and use what you learn to improve both the app and the testing process itself. ([CUNY-AI-Lab/slide-maker](https://github.com/CUNY-AI-Lab/slide-maker/blob/HEAD/tools/qa-walkthrough/agents/qa-runner.md) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **qa-runner**: Execute OSDU API tests against live environments with parallel execution support. Use proactively when the user asks to run tests, smoke tests, or full test suites against OSDU environments (azure/ship, cimpl/qa, cimpl/temp, cimpl/dev1). Not for analyzing test failures (use qa-analyzer) or comparing results (use qa-comparator). ([danielscholl/claude-osdu](https://github.com/danielscholl/claude-osdu/blob/HEAD/plugins/osdu/agents/qa-runner.md) · ★2)
- **qa-runner**: Use to execute a regression pass and report — running the swift-testing suite, parsing failures, building and launching the macOS app, driving core user flows, capturing screenshots, and summarising state of the app right now.
